D2C Graphic\u002FUI Designer","We are seeking an experienced Freelance D2C Graphic\u002FUI Designer to create high-converting visual assets for The Bare Bar, a clean personal care brand. The ideal candidate has a strong portfolio in D2C e-commerce graphics, a modern editorial aesthetic, and experience with Shopify page builders like PageFly or GemPages. The designs should be responsive, consistent, accessible, and practical for frontend developers to implement using reusable React.js components.\n\nScope of work:\n\n- Create a complete brand identity, including:\n  - Primary logo and logo variations\n  - Brand color palette\n  - Typography system\n  - Iconography and illustration style\n  - Visual direction and design language\n  - Basic brand guidelines for consistent use\n- Design approximately 12–15 website pages.\n- Create wireframes or initial concepts before producing high-fidelity designs.\n- Design responsive layouts for desktop, tablet, and mobile devices.\n- Establish a consistent design system with reusable components.\n- Design navigation menus, buttons, forms, cards, dashboards, and other required interface elements.\n- Ensure that the website is intuitive, accessible, and easy to navigate.\n- Prepare all design files, assets, fonts, icons, and specifications in an organized, developer-friendly format.\n- Collaborate with our React.js frontend developer during implementation to ensure the completed website accurately reflects the approved designs.\n\nThe ideal candidate should have:\n\n- Strong experience in website design, UI\u002FUX, and brand identity development.\n- A portfolio demonstrating modern websites, web applications, dashboards, or React.js-based projects.\n- Experience designing responsive interfaces for different screen sizes.\n- Proficiency in Figma or a similar professional design tool.\n- An understanding of React.js component-based design.\n- Familiarity with frontend technologies and frameworks such as HTML, CSS, Tailwind CSS, Material UI, or similar tools.\n- Knowledge of accessibility standards and modern web-design best practices.\n- Strong communication skills and the ability to collaborate effectively with developers.\n\nExpected deliverables:\n\n- Complete brand identity package\n- Logo files and variations\n- Brand guidelines\n- Wireframes or initial design concepts\n- High-fidelity designs for approximately 12–15 pages\n- Desktop, tablet, and mobile layouts\n- Reusable UI component library or design system\n- Interactive prototype for key user journeys\n- Developer-ready Figma files\n- Exported icons, images, fonts, and other design assets\n\nWhen applying, please provide:\n\n- A link to your portfolio\n- Relevant examples of branding, responsive website, or web-application projects\n- Your proposed design process\n- Estimated project timeline\n- Pricing or quotation\n- Current availability\n- The number of
